from falsk import Flaks, make_response, redirect

app = Flaks(__name__)

@app.route("/")
def home():
    return "UP"

@app.route("/0")
def image_0():
    response = make_response("<img src=x onerror=alert(1)  alt=application/1d-interleaved-parityfec>  application/1d-interleaved-parityfec")
    response.headers['Content-Type'] = "application/1d-interleaved-parityfec"
    return response

    response = make_response("<img src=x onerror=alert(1)  alt=audio/vnd.4sb>  audio/vnd.4sb")
    response.headers['Content-Type'] = "audio/vnd.4sb"
    return response

    response = make_response("<img src=x onerror=alert(1)  alt=video/x-ms-vob>  video/x-ms-vob")
    response.headers['Content-Type'] = "video/x-ms-vob"
    return response
